new file mode 100644

+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import { JSDOM } from 'jsdom';
+import { mountSignup } from '../signup.js';
+
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+function setup(submit) {
+  const dom = new JSDOM(html);
+  const document = dom.window.document;
+  const form = document.querySelector('form');
+  mountSignup(form, submit);
+  const field = name => form.elements.namedItem(name);
+  const fill = (values = {}) => {
+    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries({ email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'a long passphrase', confirmPassword: 'a long passphrase', ...values })) {
+      field(name).value = value;
+      field(name).dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+    }
+  };
+  const send = () => form.dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
+  return { document, form, field, fill, send, status: () => document.querySelector('[role=status]').textContent };
+}
+const tick = () => new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
+
+test('fields have labels, password autocomplete, and associated error messages', () => {
+  const { document, field } = setup();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) {
+    const input = field(name);
+    assert.ok(document.querySelector(`label[for="${input.id}"]`));
+    assert.ok(document.getElementById(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.getAttribute('aria-describedby').includes(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.required);
+  }
+  assert.equal(field('email').autocomplete, 'email');
+  assert.equal(field('password').autocomplete, 'new-password');
+  assert.equal(field('confirmPassword').type, 'password');
+});
+
+test('empty fields show errors, focus the first field, and never submit', () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+  ui.send();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.document.activeElement, ui.field('email'));
+  assert.equal(calls, 0);
+});
+
+for (const [name, value] of [['email', 'bad-address'], ['email', 'a b@example.com'], ['password', 'short'], ['confirmPassword', 'different']]) {
+  test(`rejects invalid ${name}: ${value}`, () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+    ui.fill({ [name]: value });
+    ui.send();
+    assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+    assert.equal(calls, 0);
+  });
+}
+
+test('editing invalid fields clears errors and revalidates confirmation', () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.send();
+  ui.fill();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'false');
+  ui.fill({ password: 'another passphrase' });
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+});
+
+test('submits trimmed email and unchanged password, blocks duplicates, and clears secrets after success', async () => {
+  let resolve;
+  const calls = [];
+  const ui = setup(data => { calls.push(data); return new Promise(done => { resolve = done; }); });
+  ui.fill({ email: ' ada@example.com ', password: ' spaced password ', confirmPassword: ' spaced password ' });
+  ui.send();
+  ui.send();
+  assert.deepEqual(calls, [{ email: 'ada@example.com', password: ' spaced password ' }]);
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, true);
+  resolve();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('password').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
+});
+
+test('submission failures are safe, retain input, and allow retry', async () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(async () => { if (++calls === 1) throw new Error('private backend detail'); });
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /try again/i);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(ui.status(), /private backend/);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('email').value, 'ada@example.com');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, false);
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.equal(calls, 2);
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+});
+
+test('default preview does not claim to create an account', async () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /no account was created/i);
+});

+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import { JSDOM } from 'jsdom';
+import { mountSignup } from '../signup.js';
+
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+function setup(submit) {
+  const dom = new JSDOM(html);
+  const document = dom.window.document;
+  const form = document.querySelector('form');
+  mountSignup(form, submit);
+  const field = name => form.elements.namedItem(name);
+  const fill = (values = {}) => {
+    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries({ email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'a long passphrase', confirmPassword: 'a long passphrase', ...values })) {
+      field(name).value = value;
+      field(name).dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+    }
+  };
+  const send = () => form.dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
+  return { document, form, field, fill, send, status: () => document.querySelector('[role=status]').textContent };
+}
+const tick = () => new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
+
+test('fields have labels, password autocomplete, and associated error messages', () => {
+  const { document, field } = setup();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) {
+    const input = field(name);
+    assert.ok(document.querySelector(`label[for="${input.id}"]`));
+    assert.ok(document.getElementById(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.getAttribute('aria-describedby').includes(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.required);
+  }
+  assert.equal(field('email').autocomplete, 'email');
+  assert.equal(field('password').autocomplete, 'new-password');
+  assert.equal(field('confirmPassword').type, 'password');
+});
+
+test('empty fields show errors, focus the first field, and never submit', () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+  ui.send();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.document.activeElement, ui.field('email'));
+  assert.equal(calls, 0);
+});
+
+for (const [name, value] of [['email', 'bad-address'], ['email', 'a b@example.com'], ['password', 'short'], ['confirmPassword', 'different']]) {
+  test(`rejects invalid ${name}: ${value}`, () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+    ui.fill({ [name]: value });
+    ui.send();
+    assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+    assert.equal(calls, 0);
+  });
+}
+
+test('editing invalid fields clears errors and revalidates confirmation', () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.send();
+  ui.fill();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'false');
+  ui.fill({ password: 'another passphrase' });
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+});
+
+test('submits trimmed email and unchanged password, blocks duplicates, and clears secrets after success', async () => {
+  let resolve;
+  const calls = [];
+  const ui = setup(data => { calls.push(data); return new Promise(done => { resolve = done; }); });
+  ui.fill({ email: ' ada@example.com ', password: ' spaced password ', confirmPassword: ' spaced password ' });
+  ui.send();
+  ui.send();
+  assert.deepEqual(calls, [{ email: 'ada@example.com', password: ' spaced password ' }]);
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, true);
+  resolve();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('password').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
+});
+
+test('submission failures are safe, retain input, and allow retry', async () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(async () => { if (++calls === 1) throw new Error('private backend detail'); });
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /try again/i);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(ui.status(), /private backend/);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('email').value, 'ada@example.com');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, false);
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.equal(calls, 2);
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+});
+
+test('default preview does not claim to create an account', async () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /no account was created/i);
+});

+  ui.send();
+  ui.send();

+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, true);
+  resolve();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('password').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
+});
+
+test('submission failures are safe, retain input, and allow retry', async () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(async () => { if (++calls === 1) throw new Error('private backend detail'); });
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /try again/i);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(ui.status(), /private backend/);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('email').value, 'ada@example.com');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, false);
+  ui.send();

+  assert.equal(calls, 2);
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+});

+  ui.fill();

+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /no account was created/i);

+# Sign-up form
+
+Run `npm ci`, then `npm start` and open http://127.0.0.1:3000.
+Run `npm test` for the DOM behavior tests (Node 20.19+).
+
+This standalone page validates email, an eight-character minimum password, and
+password confirmation. Errors are associated with their fields; an invalid submit
+focuses the first error. Submission disables the form and prevents duplicates.
+Failures retain input for retry; successful submissions clear password fields.
+
+The default page is explicitly a preview: it creates no account, sends no request,
+and stores no credentials. To connect your account service, replace the call in
+`main.js` with `mountSignup(form, async ({ email, password }) => { ... })`.
+The handler must resolve only after successful registration and reject on failure
+(including non-success HTTP responses). Providing a handler changes the button
+and success message and removes the preview notice.
+
+The account service must independently validate input and handle authentication
+and credential storage. `server.js` only serves the four public assets and the
+HTML page locally; it is a development preview server.

+          </div>

diff --git a/server.js b/server.js

+import { createServer } from 'node:http';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+
+const files = new Map([
+  ['/', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/index.html', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/styles.css', ['styles.css', 'text/css; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/main.js', ['main.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/signup.js', ['signup.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+]);
+const server = createServer(async (request, response) => {
+  if (!['GET', 'HEAD'].includes(request.method)) {
+    response.writeHead(405, { Allow: 'GET, HEAD' }).end();
+    return;
+  }
+  const file = files.get(request.url.split('?')[0]);
+  if (!file) { response.writeHead(404).end('Not found'); return; }
+  try {
+    const body = await readFile(new URL(file[0], import.meta.url));
+    response.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': file[1], 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
+    response.end(request.method === 'HEAD' ? undefined : body);
+  } catch {
+    response.writeHead(500).end('Unable to load page');
+  }
+});
+server.listen(Number(process.env.PORT || 3000), '127.0.0.1', () => {
+  console.log(`Sign-up preview: http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`);
+});
diff --git a/signup.js b/signup.js

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..e30355f3480da58cf38b80442afcf29e1746cb1e

+++ b/signup.js
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+/** Connect an async submit({ email, password }) handler to create real accounts.
+ * Resolve only when registration succeeds; reject on failure.
+ */
+export function mountSignup(form, submit) {
+  const names = ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword'];
+  const inputs = Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, form.elements.namedItem(name)]));
+  const button = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
+  const fieldset = form.querySelector('fieldset');
+  const status = form.querySelector('[role="status"]');
+  const preview = typeof submit !== 'function';
+  const idleLabel = preview ? 'Try sign-up form →' : 'Create account →';
+  let attempted = false;
+  let pending = false;
+
+  form.noValidate = true;
+  fieldset.disabled = false;
+  button.textContent = idleLabel;
+  if (!preview) {
+    form.ownerDocument.getElementById('preview-note')?.remove();
+    form.removeAttribute('aria-describedby');
+  }
+
+  function validate() {
+    const email = inputs.email.value.trim();
+    const password = inputs.password.value;
+    const confirmation = inputs.confirmPassword.value;
+    const errors = {
+      email: !email ? 'Enter your email address.' : !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email) ? 'Enter a valid email address.' : '',
+      password: !password ? 'Enter a password.' : password.length < 8 ? 'Use at least 8 characters.' : '',
+      confirmPassword: !confirmation ? 'Confirm your password.' : password !== confirmation ? 'Your passwords do not match.' : '',
+    };
+    for (const name of names) {
+      inputs[name].setAttribute('aria-invalid', String(Boolean(errors[name])));
+      form.querySelector(`#${name}-error`).textContent = errors[name];
+    }
+    return names.find(name => errors[name]);
+  }
+
+  form.addEventListener('input', () => {
+    if (pending) return;
+    status.textContent = '';
+    if (attempted) validate();
+  });
+
+  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    if (pending) return;
+    attempted = true;
+    const invalid = validate();
+    if (invalid) {
+      status.textContent = 'Please check the highlighted fields.';
+      inputs[invalid].focus();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    pending = true;
+    fieldset.disabled = true;
+    button.disabled = true;
+    form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
+    button.textContent = preview ? 'Checking…' : 'Creating account…';
+    status.textContent = preview ? 'Checking your details…' : 'Creating your account…';
+    try {
+      if (!preview) await submit({ email: inputs.email.value.trim(), password: inputs.password.value });
+      inputs.password.value = '';
+      inputs.confirmPassword.value = '';
+      attempted = false;
+      status.textContent = preview ? 'Your details look good. This is a preview; no account was created.' : 'Account created. You’re all set!';
+    } catch {
+      status.textContent = 'We couldn’t create your account. Please try again.';
+    } finally {
+      pending = false;
+      fieldset.disabled = false;
+      button.disabled = false;
+      form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
+      button.textContent = idleLabel;
+    }
+  });
+}

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..3ee1907035eec23eb9f7b59c409e2fd171e9fd61

@@ -0,0 +1,108 @@
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import { JSDOM } from 'jsdom';
+import { mountSignup } from '../signup.js';
+
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+function setup(submit) {
+  const dom = new JSDOM(html);
+  const document = dom.window.document;
+  const form = document.querySelector('form');
+  mountSignup(form, submit);
+  const field = name => form.elements.namedItem(name);
+  const fill = (values = {}) => {
+    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries({ email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'a long passphrase', confirmPassword: 'a long passphrase', ...values })) {
+      field(name).value = value;
+      field(name).dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+    }
+  };
+  const send = () => form.dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
+  return { document, form, field, fill, send, status: () => document.querySelector('[role=status]').textContent };
+}
+const tick = () => new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
+
+test('fields have labels, password autocomplete, and associated error messages', () => {
+  const { document, field } = setup();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) {
+    const input = field(name);
+    assert.ok(document.querySelector(`label[for="${input.id}"]`));
+    assert.ok(document.getElementById(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.getAttribute('aria-describedby').includes(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.required);
+  }
+  assert.equal(field('email').autocomplete, 'email');
+  assert.equal(field('password').autocomplete, 'new-password');
+  assert.equal(field('confirmPassword').type, 'password');
+});
+
+test('empty fields show errors, focus the first field, and never submit', () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+  ui.send();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.document.activeElement, ui.field('email'));
+  assert.equal(calls, 0);
+});
+
+for (const [name, value] of [['email', 'bad-address'], ['email', 'a b@example.com'], ['password', 'short'], ['confirmPassword', 'different']]) {
+  test(`rejects invalid ${name}: ${value}`, () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+    ui.fill({ [name]: value });
+    ui.send();
+    assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+    assert.equal(calls, 0);
+  });
+}
+
+test('editing invalid fields clears errors and revalidates confirmation', () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.send();
+  ui.fill();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'false');
+  ui.fill({ password: 'another passphrase' });
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+});
+
+test('submits trimmed email and unchanged password, blocks duplicates, and clears secrets after success', async () => {
+  let resolve;
+  const calls = [];
+  const ui = setup(data => { calls.push(data); return new Promise(done => { resolve = done; }); });
+  ui.fill({ email: ' ada@example.com ', password: ' spaced password ', confirmPassword: ' spaced password ' });
+  ui.send();
+  ui.send();
+  assert.deepEqual(calls, [{ email: 'ada@example.com', password: ' spaced password ' }]);
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, true);
+  resolve();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('password').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
+});
+
+test('submission failures are safe, retain input, and allow retry', async () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(async () => { if (++calls === 1) throw new Error('private backend detail'); });
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /try again/i);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(ui.status(), /private backend/);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('email').value, 'ada@example.com');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, false);
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.equal(calls, 2);
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+});
+
+test('default preview does not claim to create an account', async () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /no account was created/i);
+});

+import { createServer } from 'node:http';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+
+const files = new Map([
+  ['/', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/index.html', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/styles.css', ['styles.css', 'text/css; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/main.js', ['main.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/signup.js', ['signup.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+]);
+const server = createServer(async (request, response) => {
+  if (!['GET', 'HEAD'].includes(request.method)) {
+    response.writeHead(405, { Allow: 'GET, HEAD' }).end();
+    return;
+  }
+  const file = files.get(request.url.split('?')[0]);
+  if (!file) { response.writeHead(404).end('Not found'); return; }
+  try {
+    const body = await readFile(new URL(file[0], import.meta.url));
+    response.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': file[1], 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
+    response.end(request.method === 'HEAD' ? undefined : body);
+  } catch {
+    response.writeHead(500).end('Unable to load page');
+  }
+});
+server.listen(Number(process.env.PORT || 3000), '127.0.0.1', () => {
+  console.log(`Sign-up preview: http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`);
+});

+/** Connect an async submit({ email, password }) handler to create real accounts.
+ * Resolve only when registration succeeds; reject on failure.
+ */
+export function mountSignup(form, submit) {
+  const names = ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword'];
+  const inputs = Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, form.elements.namedItem(name)]));
+  const button = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
+  const fieldset = form.querySelector('fieldset');
+  const status = form.querySelector('[role="status"]');
+  const preview = typeof submit !== 'function';
+  const idleLabel = preview ? 'Try sign-up form →' : 'Create account →';
+  let attempted = false;
+  let pending = false;
+
+  form.noValidate = true;
+  fieldset.disabled = false;
+  button.textContent = idleLabel;
+  if (!preview) {
+    form.ownerDocument.getElementById('preview-note')?.remove();
+    form.removeAttribute('aria-describedby');
+  }
+
+  function validate() {
+    const email = inputs.email.value.trim();
+    const password = inputs.password.value;
+    const confirmation = inputs.confirmPassword.value;
+    const errors = {
+      email: !email ? 'Enter your email address.' : !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email) ? 'Enter a valid email address.' : '',
+      password: !password ? 'Enter a password.' : password.length < 8 ? 'Use at least 8 characters.' : '',
+      confirmPassword: !confirmation ? 'Confirm your password.' : password !== confirmation ? 'Your passwords do not match.' : '',
+    };
+    for (const name of names) {
+      inputs[name].setAttribute('aria-invalid', String(Boolean(errors[name])));
+      form.querySelector(`#${name}-error`).textContent = errors[name];
+    }
+    return names.find(name => errors[name]);
+  }
+
+  form.addEventListener('input', () => {
+    if (pending) return;
+    status.textContent = '';
+    if (attempted) validate();
+  });
+
+  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    if (pending) return;
+    attempted = true;
+    const invalid = validate();
+    if (invalid) {
+      status.textContent = 'Please check the highlighted fields.';
+      inputs[invalid].focus();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    pending = true;
+    fieldset.disabled = true;
+    button.disabled = true;
+    form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
+    button.textContent = preview ? 'Checking…' : 'Creating account…';
+    status.textContent = preview ? 'Checking your details…' : 'Creating your account…';
+    try {
+      if (!preview) await submit({ email: inputs.email.value.trim(), password: inputs.password.value });
+      inputs.password.value = '';
+      inputs.confirmPassword.value = '';
+      attempted = false;
+      status.textContent = preview ? 'Your details look good. This is a preview; no account was created.' : 'Account created. You’re all set!';
+    } catch {
+      status.textContent = 'We couldn’t create your account. Please try again.';
+    } finally {
+      pending = false;
+      fieldset.disabled = false;
+      button.disabled = false;
+      form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
+      button.textContent = idleLabel;
+    }
+  });
+}

+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import { JSDOM } from 'jsdom';
+import { mountSignup } from '../signup.js';
+
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+function setup(submit) {
+  const dom = new JSDOM(html);
+  const document = dom.window.document;
+  const form = document.querySelector('form');
+  mountSignup(form, submit);
+  const field = name => form.elements.namedItem(name);
+  const fill = (values = {}) => {
+    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries({ email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'a long passphrase', confirmPassword: 'a long passphrase', ...values })) {
+      field(name).value = value;
+      field(name).dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+    }
+  };
+  const send = () => form.dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
+  return { document, form, field, fill, send, status: () => document.querySelector('[role=status]').textContent };
+}
+const tick = () => new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
+
+test('fields have labels, password autocomplete, and associated error messages', () => {
+  const { document, field } = setup();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) {
+    const input = field(name);
+    assert.ok(document.querySelector(`label[for="${input.id}"]`));
+    assert.ok(document.getElementById(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.getAttribute('aria-describedby').includes(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.required);
+  }
+  assert.equal(field('email').autocomplete, 'email');
+  assert.equal(field('password').autocomplete, 'new-password');
+  assert.equal(field('confirmPassword').type, 'password');
+});
+
+test('empty fields show errors, focus the first field, and never submit', () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+  ui.send();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.document.activeElement, ui.field('email'));
+  assert.equal(calls, 0);
+});
+
+for (const [name, value] of [['email', 'bad-address'], ['email', 'a b@example.com'], ['password', 'short'], ['confirmPassword', 'different']]) {
+  test(`rejects invalid ${name}: ${value}`, () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+    ui.fill({ [name]: value });
+    ui.send();
+    assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+    assert.equal(calls, 0);
+  });
+}
+
+test('editing invalid fields clears errors and revalidates confirmation', () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.send();
+  ui.fill();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'false');
+  ui.fill({ password: 'another passphrase' });
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+});
+
+test('submits trimmed email and unchanged password, blocks duplicates, and clears secrets after success', async () => {
+  let resolve;
+  const calls = [];
+  const ui = setup(data => { calls.push(data); return new Promise(done => { resolve = done; }); });
+  ui.fill({ email: ' ada@example.com ', password: ' spaced password ', confirmPassword: ' spaced password ' });
+  ui.send();
+  ui.send();
+  assert.deepEqual(calls, [{ email: 'ada@example.com', password: ' spaced password ' }]);
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, true);
+  resolve();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('password').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
+});
+
+test('submission failures are safe, retain input, and allow retry', async () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(async () => { if (++calls === 1) throw new Error('private backend detail'); });
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /try again/i);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(ui.status(), /private backend/);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('email').value, 'ada@example.com');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, false);
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.equal(calls, 2);
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+});
+
+test('default preview does not claim to create an account', async () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /no account was created/i);
+});

diff --git a/README.md b/README.md

@@ -0,0 +1,20 @@
+# Sign-up form
+
+Run `npm ci`, then `npm start` and open http://127.0.0.1:3000.
+Run `npm test` for the DOM behavior tests (Node 20.19+).
+
+This standalone page validates email, an eight-character minimum password, and
+password confirmation. Errors are associated with their fields; an invalid submit
+focuses the first error. Submission disables the form and prevents duplicates.
+Failures retain input for retry; successful submissions clear password fields.
+
+The default page is explicitly a preview: it creates no account, sends no request,
+and stores no credentials. To connect your account service, replace the call in
+`main.js` with `mountSignup(form, async ({ email, password }) => { ... })`.
+The handler must resolve only after successful registration and reject on failure
+(including non-success HTTP responses). Providing a handler changes the button
+and success message and removes the preview notice.
+
+The account service must independently validate input and handle authentication
+and credential storage. `server.js` only serves the four public assets and the
+HTML page locally; it is a development preview server.

+/** Connect an async submit({ email, password }) handler to create real accounts.
+ * Resolve only when registration succeeds; reject on failure.
+ */
+export function mountSignup(form, submit) {
+  const names = ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword'];
+  const inputs = Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, form.elements.namedItem(name)]));
+  const button = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
+  const fieldset = form.querySelector('fieldset');
+  const status = form.querySelector('[role="status"]');
+  const preview = typeof submit !== 'function';
+  const idleLabel = preview ? 'Try sign-up form →' : 'Create account →';
+  let attempted = false;
+  let pending = false;

+
+  function validate() {
+    const email = inputs.email.value.trim();
+    const password = inputs.password.value;
+    const confirmation = inputs.confirmPassword.value;
+    const errors = {
+      email: !email ? 'Enter your email address.' : !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email) ? 'Enter a valid email address.' : '',
+      password: !password ? 'Enter a password.' : password.length < 8 ? 'Use at least 8 characters.' : '',
+      confirmPassword: !confirmation ? 'Confirm your password.' : password !== confirmation ? 'Your passwords do not match.' : '',
+    };
+    for (const name of names) {
+      inputs[name].setAttribute('aria-invalid', String(Boolean(errors[name])));
+      form.querySelector(`#${name}-error`).textContent = errors[name];
+    }
+    return names.find(name => errors[name]);
+  }
+
+  form.addEventListener('input', () => {
+    if (pending) return;
+    status.textContent = '';
+    if (attempted) validate();
+  });
+
+  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    if (pending) return;
+    attempted = true;
+    const invalid = validate();
+    if (invalid) {
+      status.textContent = 'Please check the highlighted fields.';
+      inputs[invalid].focus();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    pending = true;
+    fieldset.disabled = true;
+    button.disabled = true;
+    form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
+    button.textContent = preview ? 'Checking…' : 'Creating account…';
+    status.textContent = preview ? 'Checking your details…' : 'Creating your account…';
+    try {
+      if (!preview) await submit({ email: inputs.email.value.trim(), password: inputs.password.value });
+      inputs.password.value = '';
+      inputs.confirmPassword.value = '';
+      attempted = false;
+      status.textContent = preview ? 'Your details look good. This is a preview; no account was created.' : 'Account created. You’re all set!';
+    } catch {
+      status.textContent = 'We couldn’t create your account. Please try again.';
+    } finally {
+      pending = false;
+      fieldset.disabled = false;
+      button.disabled = false;
+      form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
+      button.textContent = idleLabel;
+    }
+  });
+}

+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import { JSDOM } from 'jsdom';
+import { mountSignup } from '../signup.js';
+
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+function setup(submit) {
+  const dom = new JSDOM(html);
+  const document = dom.window.document;
+  const form = document.querySelector('form');
+  mountSignup(form, submit);
+  const field = name => form.elements.namedItem(name);
+  const fill = (values = {}) => {
+    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries({ email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'a long passphrase', confirmPassword: 'a long passphrase', ...values })) {
+      field(name).value = value;
+      field(name).dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+    }
+  };
+  const send = () => form.dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
+  return { document, form, field, fill, send, status: () => document.querySelector('[role=status]').textContent };
+}
+const tick = () => new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
+
+test('fields have labels, password autocomplete, and associated error messages', () => {
+  const { document, field } = setup();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) {
+    const input = field(name);
+    assert.ok(document.querySelector(`label[for="${input.id}"]`));
+    assert.ok(document.getElementById(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.getAttribute('aria-describedby').includes(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.required);
+  }
+  assert.equal(field('email').autocomplete, 'email');
+  assert.equal(field('password').autocomplete, 'new-password');
+  assert.equal(field('confirmPassword').type, 'password');
+});
+
+test('empty fields show errors, focus the first field, and never submit', () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+  ui.send();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.document.activeElement, ui.field('email'));
+  assert.equal(calls, 0);
+});
+
+for (const [name, value] of [['email', 'bad-address'], ['email', 'a b@example.com'], ['password', 'short'], ['confirmPassword', 'different']]) {
+  test(`rejects invalid ${name}: ${value}`, () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+    ui.fill({ [name]: value });
+    ui.send();
+    assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+    assert.equal(calls, 0);
+  });
+}
+
+test('editing invalid fields clears errors and revalidates confirmation', () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.send();
+  ui.fill();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'false');
+  ui.fill({ password: 'another passphrase' });
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+});
+
+test('submits trimmed email and unchanged password, blocks duplicates, and clears secrets after success', async () => {
+  let resolve;
+  const calls = [];
+  const ui = setup(data => { calls.push(data); return new Promise(done => { resolve = done; }); });
+  ui.fill({ email: ' ada@example.com ', password: ' spaced password ', confirmPassword: ' spaced password ' });
+  ui.send();
+  ui.send();
+  assert.deepEqual(calls, [{ email: 'ada@example.com', password: ' spaced password ' }]);
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, true);
+  resolve();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('password').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
+});
+
+test('submission failures are safe, retain input, and allow retry', async () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(async () => { if (++calls === 1) throw new Error('private backend detail'); });
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /try again/i);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(ui.status(), /private backend/);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('email').value, 'ada@example.com');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, false);
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.equal(calls, 2);
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+});
+
+test('default preview does not claim to create an account', async () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /no account was created/i);
+});

diff --git a/README.md b/README.md

+# Sign-up form
+
+Run `npm ci`, then `npm start` and open http://127.0.0.1:3000.
+Run `npm test` for the DOM behavior tests (Node 20.19+).
+
+This standalone page validates email, an eight-character minimum password, and
+password confirmation. Errors are associated with their fields; an invalid submit
+focuses the first error. Submission disables the form and prevents duplicates.
+Failures retain input for retry; successful submissions clear password fields.
+
+The default page is explicitly a preview: it creates no account, sends no request,
+and stores no credentials. To connect your account service, replace the call in
+`main.js` with `mountSignup(form, async ({ email, password }) => { ... })`.
+The handler must resolve only after successful registration and reject on failure
+(including non-success HTTP responses). Providing a handler changes the button
+and success message and removes the preview notice.
+
+The account service must independently validate input and handle authentication
+and credential storage. `server.js` only serves the four public assets and the
+HTML page locally; it is a development preview server.

+import { createServer } from 'node:http';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+
+const files = new Map([
+  ['/', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/index.html', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/styles.css', ['styles.css', 'text/css; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/main.js', ['main.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/signup.js', ['signup.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+]);
+const server = createServer(async (request, response) => {
+  if (!['GET', 'HEAD'].includes(request.method)) {
+    response.writeHead(405, { Allow: 'GET, HEAD' }).end();
+    return;
+  }
+  const file = files.get(request.url.split('?')[0]);
+  if (!file) { response.writeHead(404).end('Not found'); return; }
+  try {
+    const body = await readFile(new URL(file[0], import.meta.url));
+    response.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': file[1], 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
+    response.end(request.method === 'HEAD' ? undefined : body);
+  } catch {
+    response.writeHead(500).end('Unable to load page');
+  }
+});
+server.listen(Number(process.env.PORT || 3000), '127.0.0.1', () => {
+  console.log(`Sign-up preview: http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`);
+});
diff --git a/signup.js b/signup.js

@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+/** Connect an async submit({ email, password }) handler to create real accounts.
+ * Resolve only when registration succeeds; reject on failure.
+ */
+export function mountSignup(form, submit) {
+  const names = ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword'];
+  const inputs = Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, form.elements.namedItem(name)]));
+  const button = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
+  const fieldset = form.querySelector('fieldset');
+  const status = form.querySelector('[role="status"]');
+  const preview = typeof submit !== 'function';
+  const idleLabel = preview ? 'Try sign-up form →' : 'Create account →';
+  let attempted = false;
+  let pending = false;
+
+  form.noValidate = true;
+  fieldset.disabled = false;
+  button.textContent = idleLabel;
+  if (!preview) {
+    form.ownerDocument.getElementById('preview-note')?.remove();
+    form.removeAttribute('aria-describedby');
+  }
+
+  function validate() {
+    const email = inputs.email.value.trim();
+    const password = inputs.password.value;
+    const confirmation = inputs.confirmPassword.value;
+    const errors = {
+      email: !email ? 'Enter your email address.' : !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email) ? 'Enter a valid email address.' : '',
+      password: !password ? 'Enter a password.' : password.length < 8 ? 'Use at least 8 characters.' : '',
+      confirmPassword: !confirmation ? 'Confirm your password.' : password !== confirmation ? 'Your passwords do not match.' : '',
+    };
+    for (const name of names) {
+      inputs[name].setAttribute('aria-invalid', String(Boolean(errors[name])));
+      form.querySelector(`#${name}-error`).textContent = errors[name];
+    }
+    return names.find(name => errors[name]);
+  }
+
+  form.addEventListener('input', () => {
+    if (pending) return;
+    status.textContent = '';
+    if (attempted) validate();
+  });
+
+  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    if (pending) return;
+    attempted = true;
+    const invalid = validate();
+    if (invalid) {
+      status.textContent = 'Please check the highlighted fields.';
+      inputs[invalid].focus();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    pending = true;
+    fieldset.disabled = true;
+    button.disabled = true;
+    form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
+    button.textContent = preview ? 'Checking…' : 'Creating account…';
+    status.textContent = preview ? 'Checking your details…' : 'Creating your account…';
+    try {
+      if (!preview) await submit({ email: inputs.email.value.trim(), password: inputs.password.value });
+      inputs.password.value = '';
+      inputs.confirmPassword.value = '';
+      attempted = false;
+      status.textContent = preview ? 'Your details look good. This is a preview; no account was created.' : 'Account created. You’re all set!';
+    } catch {
+      status.textContent = 'We couldn’t create your account. Please try again.';
+    } finally {
+      pending = false;
+      fieldset.disabled = false;
+      button.disabled = false;
+      form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
+      button.textContent = idleLabel;
+    }
+  });
+}

+@media (max-width: 480px) {
+  body { padding: 20px 12px; }
+  .card { padding: 28px 22px; }

+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import { JSDOM } from 'jsdom';
+import { mountSignup } from '../signup.js';
+
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+function setup(submit) {
+  const dom = new JSDOM(html);
+  const document = dom.window.document;
+  const form = document.querySelector('form');
+  mountSignup(form, submit);
+  const field = name => form.elements.namedItem(name);
+  const fill = (values = {}) => {
+    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries({ email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'a long passphrase', confirmPassword: 'a long passphrase', ...values })) {
+      field(name).value = value;
+      field(name).dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+    }
+  };
+  const send = () => form.dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
+  return { document, form, field, fill, send, status: () => document.querySelector('[role=status]').textContent };
+}
+const tick = () => new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
+
+test('fields have labels, password autocomplete, and associated error messages', () => {
+  const { document, field } = setup();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) {
+    const input = field(name);
+    assert.ok(document.querySelector(`label[for="${input.id}"]`));
+    assert.ok(document.getElementById(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.getAttribute('aria-describedby').includes(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.required);
+  }
+  assert.equal(field('email').autocomplete, 'email');
+  assert.equal(field('password').autocomplete, 'new-password');
+  assert.equal(field('confirmPassword').type, 'password');
+});
+
+test('empty fields show errors, focus the first field, and never submit', () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+  ui.send();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.document.activeElement, ui.field('email'));
+  assert.equal(calls, 0);
+});
+
+for (const [name, value] of [['email', 'bad-address'], ['email', 'a b@example.com'], ['password', 'short'], ['confirmPassword', 'different']]) {
+  test(`rejects invalid ${name}: ${value}`, () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+    ui.fill({ [name]: value });
+    ui.send();
+    assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+    assert.equal(calls, 0);
+  });
+}
+
+test('editing invalid fields clears errors and revalidates confirmation', () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.send();
+  ui.fill();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'false');
+  ui.fill({ password: 'another passphrase' });
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+});
+
+test('submits trimmed email and unchanged password, blocks duplicates, and clears secrets after success', async () => {
+  let resolve;
+  const calls = [];
+  const ui = setup(data => { calls.push(data); return new Promise(done => { resolve = done; }); });
+  ui.fill({ email: ' ada@example.com ', password: ' spaced password ', confirmPassword: ' spaced password ' });
+  ui.send();
+  ui.send();
+  assert.deepEqual(calls, [{ email: 'ada@example.com', password: ' spaced password ' }]);
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, true);
+  resolve();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('password').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
+});
+
+test('submission failures are safe, retain input, and allow retry', async () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(async () => { if (++calls === 1) throw new Error('private backend detail'); });
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /try again/i);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(ui.status(), /private backend/);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('email').value, 'ada@example.com');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, false);
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.equal(calls, 2);
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+});
+
+test('default preview does not claim to create an account', async () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /no account was created/i);
+});

diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..fcd52e3831d1239857f21b59951701b1840c50ca
--- /dev/null
+++ b/README.md
@@ -0,0 +1,20 @@
+# Sign-up form
+
+Run `npm ci`, then `npm start` and open http://127.0.0.1:3000.
+Run `npm test` for the DOM behavior tests (Node 20.19+).
+
+This standalone page validates email, an eight-character minimum password, and
+password confirmation. Errors are associated with their fields; an invalid submit
+focuses the first error. Submission disables the form and prevents duplicates.
+Failures retain input for retry; successful submissions clear password fields.
+
+The default page is explicitly a preview: it creates no account, sends no request,
+and stores no credentials. To connect your account service, replace the call in
+`main.js` with `mountSignup(form, async ({ email, password }) => { ... })`.
+The handler must resolve only after successful registration and reject on failure
+(including non-success HTTP responses). Providing a handler changes the button
+and success message and removes the preview notice.
+
+The account service must independently validate input and handle authentication
+and credential storage. `server.js` only serves the four public assets and the
+HTML page locally; it is a development preview server.

+import { createServer } from 'node:http';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+
+const files = new Map([
+  ['/', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/index.html', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/styles.css', ['styles.css', 'text/css; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/main.js', ['main.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/signup.js', ['signup.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+]);
+const server = createServer(async (request, response) => {
+  if (!['GET', 'HEAD'].includes(request.method)) {
+    response.writeHead(405, { Allow: 'GET, HEAD' }).end();
+    return;
+  }
+  const file = files.get(request.url.split('?')[0]);
+  if (!file) { response.writeHead(404).end('Not found'); return; }
+  try {
+    const body = await readFile(new URL(file[0], import.meta.url));
+    response.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': file[1], 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
+    response.end(request.method === 'HEAD' ? undefined : body);
+  } catch {
+    response.writeHead(500).end('Unable to load page');
+  }
+});
+server.listen(Number(process.env.PORT || 3000), '127.0.0.1', () => {
+  console.log(`Sign-up preview: http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`);
+});
diff --git a/signup.js b/signup.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..e30355f3480da58cf38b80442afcf29e1746cb1e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/signup.js
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+/** Connect an async submit({ email, password }) handler to create real accounts.
+ * Resolve only when registration succeeds; reject on failure.
+ */
+export function mountSignup(form, submit) {
+  const names = ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword'];
+  const inputs = Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, form.elements.namedItem(name)]));
+  const button = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
+  const fieldset = form.querySelector('fieldset');
+  const status = form.querySelector('[role="status"]');
+  const preview = typeof submit !== 'function';
+  const idleLabel = preview ? 'Try sign-up form →' : 'Create account →';
+  let attempted = false;
+  let pending = false;
+
+  form.noValidate = true;
+  fieldset.disabled = false;
+  button.textContent = idleLabel;
+  if (!preview) {
+    form.ownerDocument.getElementById('preview-note')?.remove();
+    form.removeAttribute('aria-describedby');
+  }
+
+  function validate() {
+    const email = inputs.email.value.trim();
+    const password = inputs.password.value;
+    const confirmation = inputs.confirmPassword.value;
+    const errors = {
+      email: !email ? 'Enter your email address.' : !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email) ? 'Enter a valid email address.' : '',
+      password: !password ? 'Enter a password.' : password.length < 8 ? 'Use at least 8 characters.' : '',
+      confirmPassword: !confirmation ? 'Confirm your password.' : password !== confirmation ? 'Your passwords do not match.' : '',
+    };
+    for (const name of names) {
+      inputs[name].setAttribute('aria-invalid', String(Boolean(errors[name])));
+      form.querySelector(`#${name}-error`).textContent = errors[name];
+    }
+    return names.find(name => errors[name]);
+  }
+
+  form.addEventListener('input', () => {
+    if (pending) return;
+    status.textContent = '';
+    if (attempted) validate();
+  });
+
+  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    if (pending) return;
+    attempted = true;
+    const invalid = validate();
+    if (invalid) {
+      status.textContent = 'Please check the highlighted fields.';
+      inputs[invalid].focus();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    pending = true;
+    fieldset.disabled = true;
+    button.disabled = true;
+    form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
+    button.textContent = preview ? 'Checking…' : 'Creating account…';
+    status.textContent = preview ? 'Checking your details…' : 'Creating your account…';
+    try {
+      if (!preview) await submit({ email: inputs.email.value.trim(), password: inputs.password.value });
+      inputs.password.value = '';
+      inputs.confirmPassword.value = '';
+      attempted = false;
+      status.textContent = preview ? 'Your details look good. This is a preview; no account was created.' : 'Account created. You’re all set!';
+    } catch {
+      status.textContent = 'We couldn’t create your account. Please try again.';
+    } finally {
+      pending = false;
+      fieldset.disabled = false;
+      button.disabled = false;
+      form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
+      button.textContent = idleLabel;
+    }
+  });
+}

/bin/bash -lc "node --input-type=module <<'JS'
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
const child = spawn(process.execPath, ['server.js'], { env: { ...process.env, PORT: '0' }, stdio: ['ignore', 'pipe', 'pipe'] });
const timeout = setTimeout(() => { child.kill(); process.exitCode = 1; }, 10000);
try {
  const origin = await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    child.stdout.once('data', chunk => resolve(chunk.toString().match(/http:\\/\\/\\S+/)[0]));
    child.once('error', reject);
    child.once('exit', code => reject(new Error("'`Server exited: ${code}`)));
    child.stderr.on('"'data', data => process.stderr.write(data));
  });
  for (const path of ['/', '/styles.css', '/main.js', '/signup.js']) assert.equal((await fetch(origin + path)).status, 200);
  assert.equal((await fetch(origin + '/.storytree.json')).status, 404);
  assert.equal((await fetch(origin + '/', { method: 'POST' })).status, 405);
  console.log('Server smoke checks passed: public assets, private-file rejection, POST rejection.');
} finally {
  clearTimeout(timeout);
  child.kill();
}
JS" in /home/mickh/st-live86/signup-codex

diff --git a/README.md b/README.md

+# Sign-up form
+
+Run `npm ci`, then `npm start` and open http://127.0.0.1:3000.
+Run `npm test` for the DOM behavior tests (Node 20.19+).
+
+This standalone page validates email, an eight-character minimum password, and
+password confirmation. Errors are associated with their fields; an invalid submit
+focuses the first error. Submission disables the form and prevents duplicates.
+Failures retain input for retry; successful submissions clear password fields.
+
+The default page is explicitly a preview: it creates no account, sends no request,
+and stores no credentials. To connect your account service, replace the call in
+`main.js` with `mountSignup(form, async ({ email, password }) => { ... })`.
+The handler must resolve only after successful registration and reject on failure
+(including non-success HTTP responses). Providing a handler changes the button
+and success message and removes the preview notice.
+
+The account service must independently validate input and handle authentication
+and credential storage. `server.js` only serves the four public assets and the
+HTML page locally; it is a development preview server.

diff --git a/server.js b/server.js

@@ -0,0 +1,28 @@
+import { createServer } from 'node:http';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+
+const files = new Map([
+  ['/', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/index.html', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/styles.css', ['styles.css', 'text/css; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/main.js', ['main.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/signup.js', ['signup.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+]);
+const server = createServer(async (request, response) => {
+  if (!['GET', 'HEAD'].includes(request.method)) {
+    response.writeHead(405, { Allow: 'GET, HEAD' }).end();
+    return;
+  }
+  const file = files.get(request.url.split('?')[0]);
+  if (!file) { response.writeHead(404).end('Not found'); return; }
+  try {
+    const body = await readFile(new URL(file[0], import.meta.url));
+    response.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': file[1], 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
+    response.end(request.method === 'HEAD' ? undefined : body);
+  } catch {
+    response.writeHead(500).end('Unable to load page');
+  }
+});
+server.listen(Number(process.env.PORT || 3000), '127.0.0.1', () => {
+  console.log(`Sign-up preview: http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`);
+});
diff --git a/signup.js b/signup.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..e30355f3480da58cf38b80442afcf29e1746cb1e
--- /dev/null
+++ b/signup.js
@@ -0,0 +1,78 @@
+/** Connect an async submit({ email, password }) handler to create real accounts.
+ * Resolve only when registration succeeds; reject on failure.
+ */
+export function mountSignup(form, submit) {
+  const names = ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword'];
+  const inputs = Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, form.elements.namedItem(name)]));
+  const button = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
+  const fieldset = form.querySelector('fieldset');
+  const status = form.querySelector('[role="status"]');
+  const preview = typeof submit !== 'function';
+  const idleLabel = preview ? 'Try sign-up form →' : 'Create account →';
+  let attempted = false;
+  let pending = false;
+
+  form.noValidate = true;
+  fieldset.disabled = false;
+  button.textContent = idleLabel;
+  if (!preview) {
+    form.ownerDocument.getElementById('preview-note')?.remove();
+    form.removeAttribute('aria-describedby');
+  }
+
+  function validate() {
+    const email = inputs.email.value.trim();
+    const password = inputs.password.value;
+    const confirmation = inputs.confirmPassword.value;
+    const errors = {
+      email: !email ? 'Enter your email address.' : !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email) ? 'Enter a valid email address.' : '',
+      password: !password ? 'Enter a password.' : password.length < 8 ? 'Use at least 8 characters.' : '',
+      confirmPassword: !confirmation ? 'Confirm your password.' : password !== confirmation ? 'Your passwords do not match.' : '',
+    };
+    for (const name of names) {
+      inputs[name].setAttribute('aria-invalid', String(Boolean(errors[name])));
+      form.querySelector(`#${name}-error`).textContent = errors[name];
+    }
+    return names.find(name => errors[name]);
+  }
+
+  form.addEventListener('input', () => {
+    if (pending) return;
+    status.textContent = '';
+    if (attempted) validate();
+  });
+
+  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    if (pending) return;
+    attempted = true;
+    const invalid = validate();
+    if (invalid) {
+      status.textContent = 'Please check the highlighted fields.';
+      inputs[invalid].focus();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    pending = true;
+    fieldset.disabled = true;
+    button.disabled = true;
+    form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
+    button.textContent = preview ? 'Checking…' : 'Creating account…';
+    status.textContent = preview ? 'Checking your details…' : 'Creating your account…';
+    try {
+      if (!preview) await submit({ email: inputs.email.value.trim(), password: inputs.password.value });
+      inputs.password.value = '';
+      inputs.confirmPassword.value = '';
+      attempted = false;
+      status.textContent = preview ? 'Your details look good. This is a preview; no account was created.' : 'Account created. You’re all set!';
+    } catch {
+      status.textContent = 'We couldn’t create your account. Please try again.';
+    } finally {
+      pending = false;
+      fieldset.disabled = false;
+      button.disabled = false;
+      form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
+      button.textContent = idleLabel;
+    }
+  });
+}

diff --git a/test/signup.test.js b/test/signup.test.js
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..3ee1907035eec23eb9f7b59c409e2fd171e9fd61
--- /dev/null
+++ b/test/signup.test.js
@@ -0,0 +1,108 @@
+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import { JSDOM } from 'jsdom';
+import { mountSignup } from '../signup.js';
+
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+function setup(submit) {
+  const dom = new JSDOM(html);
+  const document = dom.window.document;
+  const form = document.querySelector('form');
+  mountSignup(form, submit);
+  const field = name => form.elements.namedItem(name);
+  const fill = (values = {}) => {
+    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries({ email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'a long passphrase', confirmPassword: 'a long passphrase', ...values })) {
+      field(name).value = value;
+      field(name).dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+    }
+  };
+  const send = () => form.dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
+  return { document, form, field, fill, send, status: () => document.querySelector('[role=status]').textContent };
+}
+const tick = () => new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
+
+test('fields have labels, password autocomplete, and associated error messages', () => {
+  const { document, field } = setup();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) {
+    const input = field(name);
+    assert.ok(document.querySelector(`label[for="${input.id}"]`));
+    assert.ok(document.getElementById(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.getAttribute('aria-describedby').includes(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.required);
+  }
+  assert.equal(field('email').autocomplete, 'email');
+  assert.equal(field('password').autocomplete, 'new-password');
+  assert.equal(field('confirmPassword').type, 'password');
+});
+
+test('empty fields show errors, focus the first field, and never submit', () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+  ui.send();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.document.activeElement, ui.field('email'));
+  assert.equal(calls, 0);
+});
+
+for (const [name, value] of [['email', 'bad-address'], ['email', 'a b@example.com'], ['password', 'short'], ['confirmPassword', 'different']]) {
+  test(`rejects invalid ${name}: ${value}`, () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+    ui.fill({ [name]: value });
+    ui.send();
+    assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+    assert.equal(calls, 0);
+  });
+}
+
+test('editing invalid fields clears errors and revalidates confirmation', () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.send();
+  ui.fill();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'false');
+  ui.fill({ password: 'another passphrase' });
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+});
+
+test('submits trimmed email and unchanged password, blocks duplicates, and clears secrets after success', async () => {
+  let resolve;
+  const calls = [];
+  const ui = setup(data => { calls.push(data); return new Promise(done => { resolve = done; }); });
+  ui.fill({ email: ' ada@example.com ', password: ' spaced password ', confirmPassword: ' spaced password ' });
+  ui.send();
+  ui.send();
+  assert.deepEqual(calls, [{ email: 'ada@example.com', password: ' spaced password ' }]);
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, true);
+  resolve();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('password').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
+});
+
+test('submission failures are safe, retain input, and allow retry', async () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(async () => { if (++calls === 1) throw new Error('private backend detail'); });
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /try again/i);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(ui.status(), /private backend/);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('email').value, 'ada@example.com');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, false);
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.equal(calls, 2);
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+});
+
+test('default preview does not claim to create an account', async () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /no account was created/i);
+});

+import { createServer } from 'node:http';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+
+const files = new Map([
+  ['/', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/index.html', ['index.html', 'text/html; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/styles.css', ['styles.css', 'text/css; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/main.js', ['main.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+  ['/signup.js', ['signup.js', 'text/javascript; charset=utf-8']],
+]);
+const server = createServer(async (request, response) => {
+  if (!['GET', 'HEAD'].includes(request.method)) {
+    response.writeHead(405, { Allow: 'GET, HEAD' }).end();
+    return;
+  }
+  const file = files.get(request.url.split('?')[0]);
+  if (!file) { response.writeHead(404).end('Not found'); return; }
+  try {
+    const body = await readFile(new URL(file[0], import.meta.url));
+    response.writeHead(200, { 'Content-Type': file[1], 'Cache-Control': 'no-store' });
+    response.end(request.method === 'HEAD' ? undefined : body);
+  } catch {
+    response.writeHead(500).end('Unable to load page');
+  }
+});
+server.listen(Number(process.env.PORT || 3000), '127.0.0.1', () => {
+  console.log(`Sign-up preview: http://127.0.0.1:${server.address().port}`);
+});
diff --git a/signup.js b/signup.js

+/** Connect an async submit({ email, password }) handler to create real accounts.
+ * Resolve only when registration succeeds; reject on failure.
+ */
+export function mountSignup(form, submit) {
+  const names = ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword'];
+  const inputs = Object.fromEntries(names.map(name => [name, form.elements.namedItem(name)]));
+  const button = form.querySelector('button[type="submit"]');
+  const fieldset = form.querySelector('fieldset');
+  const status = form.querySelector('[role="status"]');
+  const preview = typeof submit !== 'function';
+  const idleLabel = preview ? 'Try sign-up form →' : 'Create account →';
+  let attempted = false;
+  let pending = false;
+
+  form.noValidate = true;
+  fieldset.disabled = false;
+  button.textContent = idleLabel;
+  if (!preview) {
+    form.ownerDocument.getElementById('preview-note')?.remove();
+    form.removeAttribute('aria-describedby');
+  }
+
+  function validate() {
+    const email = inputs.email.value.trim();
+    const password = inputs.password.value;
+    const confirmation = inputs.confirmPassword.value;
+    const errors = {
+      email: !email ? 'Enter your email address.' : !/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email) ? 'Enter a valid email address.' : '',
+      password: !password ? 'Enter a password.' : password.length < 8 ? 'Use at least 8 characters.' : '',
+      confirmPassword: !confirmation ? 'Confirm your password.' : password !== confirmation ? 'Your passwords do not match.' : '',
+    };
+    for (const name of names) {
+      inputs[name].setAttribute('aria-invalid', String(Boolean(errors[name])));
+      form.querySelector(`#${name}-error`).textContent = errors[name];
+    }
+    return names.find(name => errors[name]);
+  }
+
+  form.addEventListener('input', () => {
+    if (pending) return;
+    status.textContent = '';
+    if (attempted) validate();
+  });
+
+  form.addEventListener('submit', async event => {
+    event.preventDefault();
+    if (pending) return;
+    attempted = true;
+    const invalid = validate();
+    if (invalid) {
+      status.textContent = 'Please check the highlighted fields.';
+      inputs[invalid].focus();
+      return;
+    }
+
+    pending = true;
+    fieldset.disabled = true;
+    button.disabled = true;
+    form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'true');
+    button.textContent = preview ? 'Checking…' : 'Creating account…';
+    status.textContent = preview ? 'Checking your details…' : 'Creating your account…';
+    try {
+      if (!preview) await submit({ email: inputs.email.value.trim(), password: inputs.password.value });
+      inputs.password.value = '';
+      inputs.confirmPassword.value = '';
+      attempted = false;
+      status.textContent = preview ? 'Your details look good. This is a preview; no account was created.' : 'Account created. You’re all set!';
+    } catch {
+      status.textContent = 'We couldn’t create your account. Please try again.';
+    } finally {
+      pending = false;
+      fieldset.disabled = false;
+      button.disabled = false;
+      form.setAttribute('aria-busy', 'false');
+      button.textContent = idleLabel;
+    }
+  });
+}

+import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
+import { readFile } from 'node:fs/promises';
+import { test } from 'node:test';
+import { JSDOM } from 'jsdom';
+import { mountSignup } from '../signup.js';
+
+const html = await readFile(new URL('../index.html', import.meta.url), 'utf8');
+function setup(submit) {
+  const dom = new JSDOM(html);
+  const document = dom.window.document;
+  const form = document.querySelector('form');
+  mountSignup(form, submit);
+  const field = name => form.elements.namedItem(name);
+  const fill = (values = {}) => {
+    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries({ email: 'ada@example.com', password: 'a long passphrase', confirmPassword: 'a long passphrase', ...values })) {
+      field(name).value = value;
+      field(name).dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('input', { bubbles: true }));
+    }
+  };
+  const send = () => form.dispatchEvent(new dom.window.Event('submit', { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
+  return { document, form, field, fill, send, status: () => document.querySelector('[role=status]').textContent };
+}
+const tick = () => new Promise(resolve => setImmediate(resolve));
+
+test('fields have labels, password autocomplete, and associated error messages', () => {
+  const { document, field } = setup();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) {
+    const input = field(name);
+    assert.ok(document.querySelector(`label[for="${input.id}"]`));
+    assert.ok(document.getElementById(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.getAttribute('aria-describedby').includes(`${name}-error`));
+    assert.ok(input.required);
+  }
+  assert.equal(field('email').autocomplete, 'email');
+  assert.equal(field('password').autocomplete, 'new-password');
+  assert.equal(field('confirmPassword').type, 'password');
+});
+
+test('empty fields show errors, focus the first field, and never submit', () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+  ui.send();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.document.activeElement, ui.field('email'));
+  assert.equal(calls, 0);
+});
+
+for (const [name, value] of [['email', 'bad-address'], ['email', 'a b@example.com'], ['password', 'short'], ['confirmPassword', 'different']]) {
+  test(`rejects invalid ${name}: ${value}`, () => {
+    let calls = 0;
+    const ui = setup(() => calls++);
+    ui.fill({ [name]: value });
+    ui.send();
+    assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+    assert.equal(calls, 0);
+  });
+}
+
+test('editing invalid fields clears errors and revalidates confirmation', () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.send();
+  ui.fill();
+  for (const name of ['email', 'password', 'confirmPassword']) assert.equal(ui.field(name).getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'false');
+  ui.fill({ password: 'another passphrase' });
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').getAttribute('aria-invalid'), 'true');
+});
+
+test('submits trimmed email and unchanged password, blocks duplicates, and clears secrets after success', async () => {
+  let resolve;
+  const calls = [];
+  const ui = setup(data => { calls.push(data); return new Promise(done => { resolve = done; }); });
+  ui.fill({ email: ' ada@example.com ', password: ' spaced password ', confirmPassword: ' spaced password ' });
+  ui.send();
+  ui.send();
+  assert.deepEqual(calls, [{ email: 'ada@example.com', password: ' spaced password ' }]);
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'true');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, true);
+  resolve();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('password').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.field('confirmPassword').value, '');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.getAttribute('aria-busy'), 'false');
+});
+
+test('submission failures are safe, retain input, and allow retry', async () => {
+  let calls = 0;
+  const ui = setup(async () => { if (++calls === 1) throw new Error('private backend detail'); });
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /try again/i);
+  assert.doesNotMatch(ui.status(), /private backend/);
+  assert.equal(ui.field('email').value, 'ada@example.com');
+  assert.equal(ui.form.querySelector('button[type=submit]').disabled, false);
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.equal(calls, 2);
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /account created/i);
+});
+
+test('default preview does not claim to create an account', async () => {
+  const ui = setup();
+  ui.fill();
+  ui.send();
+  await tick();
+  assert.match(ui.status(), /no account was created/i);
+});

diff --git a/README.md b/README.md
new file mode 100644

+++ b/README.md

+# Sign-up form
+
+Run `npm ci`, then `npm start` and open http://127.0.0.1:3000.
+Run `npm test` for the DOM behavior tests (Node 20.19+).
+
+This standalone page validates email, an eight-character minimum password, and
+password confirmation. Errors are associated with their fields; an invalid submit
+focuses the first error. Submission disables the form and prevents duplicates.
+Failures retain input for retry; successful submissions clear password fields.
+
+The default page is explicitly a preview: it creates no account, sends no request,
+and stores no credentials. To connect your account service, replace the call in
+`main.js` with `mountSignup(form, async ({ email, password }) => { ... })`.
+The handler must resolve only after successful registration and reject on failure
+(including non-success HTTP responses). Providing a handler changes the button
+and success message and removes the preview notice.
+
+The account service must independently validate input and handle authentication
+and credential storage. `server.js` only serves the four public assets and the
+HTML page locally; it is a development preview server.
